table inet wg_firewall {
	chain input {
		type filter hook input priority filter; policy accept;
		ct state established,related accept
		iif "lo" accept
		iifname "wg0" ip saddr 10.10.0.1 tcp dport { 80, 443 } accept
		iifname "wg0" tcp dport 22 accept
		ip saddr 10.10.0.0/24 tcp dport 22 accept
		tcp dport 22 drop
	}

	chain forward {
		type filter hook forward priority filter; policy accept;
		ct state established,related accept
	}
}

	}

	chain KUBE-PROXY-CANARY {
	}
}
table ip filter {
	chain INPUT {
		type filter hook input priority filter; policy accept;
		xt match "comment" counter packets 5407 bytes 1602488 jump KUBE-ROUTER-INPUT
		xt match "conntrack" xt match "comment" counter packets 233 bytes 17912 jump KUBE-PROXY-FIREWALL
		xt match "comment" counter packets 3984 bytes 1349390 jump KUBE-NODEPORTS
		xt match "conntrack" xt match "comment" counter packets 233 bytes 17912 jump KUBE-EXTERNAL-SERVICES
		counter packets 3984 bytes 1349390 jump KUBE-FIREWALL
		xt match "comment" meta mark & 0x00020000 == 0x00020000 counter packets 0 bytes 0 accept
	}

	chain FORWARD {
		type filter hook forward priority filter; policy accept;
		xt match "comment" counter packets 1280 bytes 906080 jump KUBE-ROUTER-FORWARD
		xt match "conntrack" xt match "comment" counter packets 922 bytes 84098 jump KUBE-PROXY-FIREWALL
		xt match "comment" counter packets 922 bytes 84098 jump KUBE-FORWARD
		xt match "conntrack" xt match "comment" counter packets 922 bytes 84098 jump KUBE-SERVICES
		xt match "conntrack" xt match "comment" counter packets 922 bytes 84098 jump KUBE-EXTERNAL-SERVICES
		xt match "comment" meta mark & 0x00020000 == 0x00020000 counter packets 52 bytes 5112 accept
		xt match "comment" counter packets 870 bytes 78986 jump FLANNEL-FWD
	}

	chain OUTPUT {
		type filter hook output priority filter; policy accept;
		xt match "comment" counter packets 5377 bytes 2037678 jump KUBE-ROUTER-OUTPUT
		xt match "conntrack" xt match "comment" counter packets 142 bytes 9777 jump KUBE-PROXY-FIREWALL
		xt match "conntrack" xt match "comment" counter packets 142 bytes 9777 jump KUBE-SERVICES
